# B8: Outgroup-divergence sweep

Per-cell ancestral-allele accuracy as a function of the deepest
outgroup's divergence depth, the relative spacing of the three
outgroups on the ladder, and how many of those outgroups the
inference sees. Same demographic skeleton as B6 (ingroup + three
outgroups) — only the three outgroup-split times move, and the
``n_out`` axis subsets the same simulated tree sequence: 3 keeps all
three outgroups, 2 keeps the shallowest and the deepest, 1 keeps the
middle one. Three inference modes per cell: ARG-based (full ts),
fixed-tree (VCF, ``FixedTreeInference`` + Kingman prior) and
local-tree (``LocalTreeInference``, genealogies inferred from the
genotypes alone). Accuracy is over **ingroup-polymorphic sites
only**, matching B6's convention.

**Simulator settings (shared)**

- ``n_ingroup`` = 20
- ``length`` = 1000000.0
- ``mu`` = 1.25e-08
- ``rec_rate`` = 1e-08
- ``pop_size`` = 30000.0
- ``ingroup_pop_size`` = 30000.0
- ``seed`` = 42
- ``depths`` = ['1e4', '1.6e4', '2.5e4', '4e4', '6.3e4', '1e5', '1.6e5', '2.5e5', '4e5', '6.3e5', '1e6', '1.6e6', '2.5e6', '4e6', '6.3e6', '1e7', '1.6e7', '2.5e7']
- ``spacings`` = ['linear', 'geometric', 'compact_near', 'compact_far']
- ``n_outs`` = [1, 2, 3]

**Spacing strategies (deepest split = ``d``)**

- ``linear``: (0.333·d, 0.667·d, 1·d)
- ``geometric``: (0.111·d, 0.333·d, 1·d)
- ``compact_near``: (0.1·d, 0.2·d, 1·d)
- ``compact_far``: (0.667·d, 0.833·d, 1·d)

## ARG mode — accuracy by (depth, spacing, n_out)

Accuracy on ingroup-polymorphic sites; ``n_poly`` is the number of such sites used. ``n_out`` is the number of outgroup populations exposed to the inference, and ``splits`` lists only the retained ones. ``baseline agree`` is the fraction of sites where Ancestree's MAP matches ``MajorityOutgroupInference``.

## VCF mode — accuracy by (depth, spacing, n_out)

Accuracy on ingroup-polymorphic sites; ``n_poly`` is the number of such sites used. ``n_out`` is the number of outgroup populations exposed to the inference, and ``splits`` lists only the retained ones. ``baseline agree`` is the fraction of sites where Ancestree's MAP matches ``MajorityOutgroupInference``.

## LOCAL_TREE mode — accuracy by (depth, spacing, n_out)

Accuracy on ingroup-polymorphic sites; ``n_poly`` is the number of such sites used. ``n_out`` is the number of outgroup populations exposed to the inference, and ``splits`` lists only the retained ones. ``baseline agree`` is the fraction of sites where Ancestree's MAP matches ``MajorityOutgroupInference``.

## Baseline-MAP agreement (`MajorityOutgroupInference`)

The ``baseline agree`` column above is the fraction of
ingroup-polymorphic sites where Ancestree's MAP allele matches the
outgroup-majority rule (the
:class:`~ancestree.inference.MajorityOutgroupInference` baseline). High
agreement = the polariser signal dominates and the model + Kingman
prior layers are minimally biasing the call. Low agreement = the
model is moving posteriors away from the simple-outgroup-vote
signal — under a well-specified likelihood low agreement is a flag
for model misspecification (or for the prior shifting low-MAC bins
where the outgroup vote is ambiguous).
